        Idea:
Cleanup and quality pass on timeline agent + gallery selection system. Three objectives: (1) ENGINEERING QUALITY: Review and refactor all code touched across the three recent features (timeline media selection context, agent task creation, gallery selection + multi-drag). Ensure excellent modular decomposition, clear responsibility boundaries, right levels of abstraction, no confusion about what happens where. This spans: useSelectedMediaClips, GallerySelectionContext, useLassoSelection, useModifierKeys, AgentChat attachment merging, useAgentSession send pipeline, create-task tool, clips.ts, generation.ts, tool-calls.ts, loop.ts, prompts.ts, useExternalDrop multi-drag, dragDrop.ts multi-drag type, MediaGalleryItem selection/drag changes, GenerationsPaneGallery selection wiring. (2) BUG FIX: Gallery selection highlighting (ring-2 ring-sky-400) is broken — it appears inconsistently, sometimes shows for a second then disappears, disappears on hover even when item is clicked/selected. This points to some state management or CSS issue in how isSelected prop flows through MediaGalleryItem or how the selection state interacts with hover/interaction states. Investigate and fix. (3) FEATURE: Make the AI timeline agent panel show over the locked pane state as well (currently it only shows when pane is unlocked).

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: The ring flashes briefly then disappears — seems to show for a moment then go away, especially on hover. Happens across item types. Q2: Same bottom-right viewport position as on full editor page — it should feel like it's always available regardless of pane state. Q3: No freezes or restrictions — we just deployed the edge function successfully. Q4: Same file is fine to minimize churn, agreed.

Three objectives: (1) ENGINEERING QUALITY: Review and refactor all code touched across the three recent features (timeline media selection context, agent task creation, gallery selection + multi-drag). Ensure excellent modular decomposition, clear responsibility boundaries, right levels of abstraction, no confusion about what happens where. This spans: useSelectedMediaClips, GallerySelectionContext, useLassoSelection, useModifierKeys, AgentChat attachment merging, useAgentSession send pipeline, create-task tool, clips.ts, generation.ts, tool-calls.ts, loop.ts, prompts.ts, useExternalDrop multi-drag, dragDrop.ts multi-drag type, MediaGalleryItem selection/drag changes, GenerationsPaneGallery selection wiring. (2) BUG FIX: Gallery selection highlighting (ring-2 ring-sky-400) is broken — it appears inconsistently, sometimes shows for a second then disappears, disappears on hover even when item is clicked/selected. This points to some state management or CSS issue in how isSelected prop flows through MediaGalleryItem or how the selection state interacts with hover/interaction states. Investigate and fix. (3) FEATURE: Make the AI timeline agent panel show over the locked pane state as well (currently it only shows when pane is unlocked).

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: The ring flashes briefly then disappears — seems to show for a moment then go away, especially on hover. Happens across item types. Q2: Same bottom-right viewport position as on full editor page — it should feel like it's always available regardless of pane state. Q3: No freezes or restrictions — we just deployed the edge function successfully. Q4: Same file is fine to minimize churn, agreed.
